- **aube-bump**: Bump nub's vendored aube engine to a newer jdx/aube upstream. Encodes the venue-less merge (build an ephemeral merge commit from nub's own objects with `git commit-tree`; no clone, no `nub-fork`, no push-back) and the two-conflict-list methodology: git's markers find TEXTUAL conflicts, the compiler finds SEMANTIC ones, both lists are machine-generated and exhaustive, so you never hand-review a diff. - **AUC**: This evaluation probes the capability of machine learning classifiers to distinguish signal events from background noise in high-energy particle physics simulations. It measures how well different algorithms and feature sets rank positive (signal) instances higher than negative (background) ones across varying data statistics. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute AUC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/AUC) · ★3)
- **auc-against-ground-truth-evaluation**: Use when a method's ability to detect differential regulation (or other binary labels) must be quantified on simulated data with known ground truth, and compared to baseline/alternative methods via AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-against-ground-truth-evaluation))
- **auc-based-signature-performance-thresholding**: Use when you have a muSignAl output table of candidate signatures (e.g. sign_cvd2.xlsx) with per-signature AUC values and need to select the high-performing subset above a specified AUC threshold such as 0.95. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/auc-based-signature-performance-thresholding))
- **auc-benchmark-comparison-across-predictors**: Use when two or more continuous variant scores (e.g. |ΔΨ|, SPIDEX ΔΨ, MaxEntScan |ΔMES|) must be compared for binary prioritization power on the same labeled mutations, with significance testing and scope-stratified subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/auc-benchmark-comparison-across-predictors))
- **auc-classification-evaluation**: Use when you have per-cell gene set scores and a binary cell-level label (treated/control, injured/control, cell-type vs rest) and need a single threshold-free metric of class separation, typically to test whether nearest-neighbor smoothing improved discrimination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-classification-evaluation))
- **auc-comparison-across-datasets**: Use when you have a continuous risk score plus one or more competing biomarkers measured on the same samples in multiple immunotherapy cohorts with a binary response column ('res'), and need a per-cohort AUC comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/auc-comparison-across-datasets))
- **auc-computation-for-gene-signatures**: Use when a fixed gene signature from TCGA RNA-seq data (tumor vs normal labels) must be scored for classification performance, and you need a concrete AUC value per cancer type as the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-computation-for-gene-signatures))
- **auc-confidence-interval-estimation**: Use when cross-validating taxonomic classifiers (e.g., Deblur/Greengenes genus-level ASV tables from different 16S hypervariable regions) against a labeled mock-community control and you need AUC with a 0.95 confidence interval to rank region performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/auc-confidence-interval-estimation))
- **auc-confound-guard**: AUC 結論三關 confound 驗證協議 — within-group OLS + AF-bin 交叉 + permutation test。宣告任何 AUC&gt;0.58 的特徵顯著性前必經此流程。觸發：「feature AUC」「discriminative」「predictive power」「TP vs FP 區分」「residualize」。 ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/2026/05/20260509_codex_nested_skills_duplicate/nested_skills_duplicate/auc-confound-guard) · ★1)
- **auc-confound-guard**: AUC 結論三關 confound 驗證協議 — within-group OLS + AF-bin 交叉 + permutation test。宣告任何 AUC&gt;0.58 的特徵顯著性前必經此流程。觸發：「feature AUC」「discriminative」「predictive power」「TP vs FP 區分」「residualize」「confound」「特徵能否區分 TP/FP」。SKIP WHEN AUC&lt;0.58 不需宣告顯著性、純描述性統計（無 TP/FP 分類目標）、未提及 AUC 的特徵觀察、bug fix / build / commit、純 docs 編輯。 ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/auc-confound-guard) · ★1)

- **auc-curve-computation-across-replicates**: Use when benchmarking rare-cell detection sensitivity on scRNA-seq data by sweeping the number of DE genes (1–144) separating a known rare population from an abundant one, computing per-replicate AUCs and averaging across ~100 synthetic replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-curve-computation-across-replicates))
- **auc-distribution-comparison-across-methods**: Use when several gene/feature-selection methods must be ranked on the same synthetic datasets with known ground truth, e.g. simulated 20,000 gene x 2,000 cell NB/ZINB count matrices with embedded impure subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-distribution-comparison-across-methods))
- **auc-estimation-and-annotation**: Use when binary classification performance for an omics predictor must be visualized as an ROC curve with an AUC legend entry, especially by users without programming skills. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/auc-estimation-and-annotation))
- **auc-evaluation-of-type-detection**: Use when query data are expected to contain cell types absent from a Symphony reference (e.g., ablated or held-out type scenarios) and you need to quantify how well mapping metrics flag those cells as novel rather than misannotating them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-evaluation-of-type-detection))
- **auc-f1-model-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing trained Z-Calling model configurations (k-mer size or full-ZA vs mixed-AZ training data) on filtered PacBio CCS reads, where per-dataset AUC and F1 scores are needed to justify a model choice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/auc-f1-model-benchmark-evaluation))
- **auc-feature-table-extraction-from-qc-runs**: Use when TARDIS targeted peak integration of raw LC–MS files (e.g. MetaboLights MTBLS8735 or cohort runs) has completed and you need the per-target, per-run AUC data.frame and QC-averaged metric tibble as a processed feature table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/auc-feature-table-extraction-from-qc-runs))
- **auc-metric-calculation**: Use when when you have computed true positive rate (TPR) and false positive rate (FPR) values across classification probability thresholds from predicted probabilities and true labels, and need to generate a single aggregate performance metric for model comparison or reporting in omics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/auc-metric-calculation))
- **auc-metric-computation**: Use when a ranked, score-filtered list of candidate target regions (e.g. from adaptive_liftover plus gr_candidate_filter) must be evaluated against matched orthologous ground-truth regions, or when tuning a window-size parameter via AUROC/AUPR in leave-one-out cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/auc-metric-computation))
- **auc-model-comparison-analysis**: Use when several classifiers have been trained on an identical labeled training split and the goal is a fair, ranked comparison of discrimination performance via AUC on a held-out test set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/auc-model-comparison-analysis))
- **auc-model-evaluation**: Use when repeated subsample models (e.g. Elastic Net glmnet fits) must be scored for predictive performance and filtered on AUC &gt; 0.5 before downstream feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/auc-model-evaluation))
- **auc-performance-comparison**: Use when benchmarking communication-inference tools (e.g., MDIC3 vs CellChat, CellPhoneDB, iTALK) against a curated literature-supported set of interactions to rank methods by support rate and AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-performance-comparison))
- **auc-prc-performance-computation**: Use when a set of ranked predicted regulatory interactions (e.g., gene–peak pairs) needs to be evaluated against a trusted positive set such as cis-eQTLs to quantify prediction performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/auc-prc-performance-computation))
- **auc-precision-metric-computation**: Use when multiple CCI prediction methods (e.g. DeepCCI, SingleCellSignalR, iTALK, CellPhoneDB, CellChat, CellCall, CytoTalk, NATMI) have produced predicted CCI lists on the same dataset and you must quantify accuracy via AUC and precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-precision-metric-computation))
- **auc-ranking-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating DEG detection methods on simulated expression datasets that carry ground-truth DE labels, and you need per-method AUC over ranked significance scores to compare detection performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-ranking-metric-computation))
- **auc-roc-discrimination-analysis**: Use when comparing probabilistic action predictors (e.g., an LLM policy vs. uncalibrated LLM, unguided RL, or prior work) and you need discrimination plus calibration metrics (ECE, Brier Score, AUC-ROC) consolidated into a comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/auc-roc-discrimination-analysis))
- **auc-roc-monte-carlo-estimation**: Use when quantifying how sensitivity of a rareness scorer (e.g. FiRE scores) varies with the number of DE genes distinguishing a ~5% minor subpopulation, requiring mean AUC-ROC over many simulated replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auc-roc-monte-carlo-estimation))
- **auc-roc-performance-evaluation**: Use when comparing BGC detectors or any sequence-labeling classifier on held-out data and you need a threshold-independent performance metric (AUC ROC) to quantify detection accuracy, e.g., generalization to unseen classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/auc-roc-performance-evaluation))
- **auc-tabulation-across-mass-windows**: Use when you need to test whether a threshold-based candidate filtering metric (e.g., ΔCCS between predicted and measured CCS) remains effective as candidate-list mass tolerance tightens from 10 ppm down to the isobaric 0 ppm case. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/auc-tabulation-across-mass-windows))
- **auc-threshold-signature-filtering**: Use when a signature-search output (e.g. muSignAl's sign_cvd2.xlsx) lists many feature panels with performance scores and you must retain only those above a defined AUC threshold and profile feature recurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/auc-threshold-signature-filtering))
- **aucell-activity-scoring**: Use when you need per-cell activity scores for gene-regulatory modules (one regulator + its targets) to build a cell-specific GRN from single-cell expression data, e.g. in the pySCENIC pipeline on dyngen simulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-activity-scoring))
- **aucell-and-binarization**: Score pySCENIC regulon or gene-signature activity with AUCell, derive thresholds, binarize AUC matrices, inspect activity plots, and compute regulon specificity scores. - **aucell-gene-set-enrichment-scoring**: Use when you have a normalized UMI matrix in SingCellaR and pre-curated lineage signature gene sets (GMT) and need enrichment-based per-cell cell-type annotation rather than marker-gene-only labeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-gene-set-enrichment-scoring))
- **aucell-gene-set-scoring-per-cell**: Use when you have single-cell/snRNA-seq expression data plus a partitioned gene regulatory network (e.g., Leiden subnetworks from a SCING GRN) and need per-cell module activity scores for phenotypic association or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-gene-set-scoring-per-cell))
- **aucell-gene-signature-enrichment-scoring**: Use when single-cell expression data (SingleCellExperiment) must be converted into per-cell gene-signature enrichment scores, e.g. as classifier features, for state assignment, or for labeling Treg/CD4T/NKT/non-CD8 cells by curated signature enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-gene-signature-enrichment-scoring))
- **aucell-gene-signature-scoring**: Use when you need per-cell gene set activity scores (e.g., malignant up/down signature activity) to calibrate cell labels, define malignant clusters, or establish normal-cell baselines in scRNA-seq or spatial transcriptomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-gene-signature-scoring))
- **aucell-module-activity-scoring**: Use when you have regulon-like gene modules (RGMs.csv) detected on a cell-lineage-specific GRN and need per-cell module activity scores over the scRNA-seq expression matrix for clustering or benchmarking against SCENIC regulons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-module-activity-scoring))
- **aucell-module-score-embedding**: Use when a SCING GRN has been built and you need cell-level embeddings for clustering, phenotypic association, or batch-effect-corrected dimensionality reduction driven by regulatory modules rather than raw genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucell-module-score-embedding))

- **aucroc-aggregation-statistics**: Use when validating that a multiplex network carries functional signal: you have curated gene sets, an RWR_CV cross-validation result table, and (optionally) rewired control networks, and need mean AUROC/AUPRC summaries to compare real vs. randomized rankings. - **aucroc-aupr-performance-evaluation**: Use when evaluating a CSNI method (e.g. LIONESS, SSN, pySCENIC) against dyngen's cell-specific ground-truth regulatory networks and you need comparable AUROC/AUPR scores per dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucroc-aupr-performance-evaluation))
- **aucroc-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when evaluating a cell-proportion or differential-abundance method's performance across simulated dataset sizes — e.g. choosing how many pseudo-replicates Scanpro's bootstrapping should use for a given total cell count per condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucroc-benchmark-evaluation))
- **aucroc-classifier-evaluation**: Use when a set of high-weight pathways from a disease-related module must be validated as a predictive signature: bulk RNA-seq expression matrices with binary phenotype labels (e.g., MMR/MSI status) are available and held-out + external-cohort AUROC is the acceptance criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucroc-classifier-evaluation))
- **aucroc-detection-discrimination-evaluation**: Use when you have predicted miRNA expression values from mRNA profiles (e.g., miRSCAPE output) plus ground-truth detection calls (detected/non-zero vs undetected) per sample or cell type, and you want an auROC-based accuracy metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/aucroc-detection-discrimination-evaluation))
